## 1-Product Overview

来源文件：`1-Product Overview_原文.docx`

1-Product Overview_原文

### 4

Welcome In this video, we will provide an overview of the SAP Emarsys customer engagement platform. As part of Sap customer experience, we support your business to deliver profitable marketing outcomes by providing personalized experiences across multiple channels, Emarsys receives data from various sources. The data collected is used for segmentation, personalization, marketing automation, AI calculation, analytics, and more. With all this data, you can easily identify and reach your target audience, acquire, retain, or win customers back via various channels, and build a loyal customer base. Are you ready for an overview? The common challenge of winning back defecting customers will be used as a narrative throughout this to help understand.

### 5

发言人 02:01

### 6

Most of our marketers start by creating an email campaign using the visual content editor, also known as VCE Emarsys comes with presbuteros email templates, but you can also use your own. If you have multiple accounts, you can also share templates and campaigns. If your business operates in different countries, you can easily create multi language campaigns. To personalize your content with content specific data, create placeholders, also called tokens. You can use these tokens across multiple channels.

### 7

发言人 02:38

_重复来源：Imp07_原文.docx_

### 8

In our winback use case, I send defecting customers and email campaign with a voucher code. I could use this voucher in a mobile wallet campaign and link the past campaigns token in this email.

发言人 02:52

_重复来源：2-Deliverability_原文.docx_

### 10

I have various options for personalization to make this email more relevant. I drag and drop a new block in my email to include product recommendations. Then I insert a pre-built recommendation logic. However, I could also build my own personalization rule and selected as a product source in my campaign. I have loyalty enabled, so I include a loyalty block to ensure that my defecting customers are aware of their membership status. I check how this email would look for my contact by using Contact Preview. I have everything loyalty related information, personalization, voucher code, and product recommendations.

### 11

发言人 03:41

Now let's see how we can automate this email.

### 13

发言人 03:44

### 14

Both Automation Center and interactions are used to automate marketing activities. The Automation Center is a proactive tool more suitable for lifecycle engagement programs like a birthday or a retention campaign. We use interactions for real time responses to events and actions, for example, to send delivery status updates or prevent a visitor from leaving your web shop without purchasing via a web channel campaign.

### 15

发言人 04:12

_重复来源：new20+21-mobile engagement management_原文.docx_

### 16

If you don't want to start from scratch, go to Tactics to generate pre-built programs. In our current use case, I select the winback defecting customers tactic. Since I have a lot of data already, I want to include AI segments in my program. So I select this option and choose all the available channels for contacting effect and customers. As you can see, the winback tactic is a program consisting of different nodes, each responsible for a specific channel or action. All programs require an entry point. In our case, it's scheduled to run every day to check who is about to churn.

### 17

发言人 04:52

### 18

To determine the target audience of your campaigns, you create segments. Segments can be based on contact, email behavior, geolocation, and customer lifecycle data. You can also build relational segments by using your own business specific data stored in external databases. AI segments use prediction scores to calculate how likely your contacts will act. So, for example, you can target contacts with high engagement probability data collected by various channels, or the Emarsys data collection script webextension also be used for segmentation. In my win back tactic I'll use this pre-screened segment, though I always have the flexibility to modify or replace it.

### 19

发言人 05:36

### 20

Now let's configure the channels. In this program, there are nodes for email, web channel and digital ads campaigns. You can use the tactics placeholders as inspiration for your messages. As I have already created my winback email, I simply selected on the email node.

### 21

发言人 05:57

### 22

Let's move to digital ads. With this channel, you can target your contacts via different social and advertising networks such as Facebook and Google Ads with data driven advertising campaigns. Here, I add a previously created digital ads audience to the corresponding node in the program to target your customers while they're on your website, use Web Channel In Web Channel's content editor, I add this image offering defecting customers a discount for their next purchase. You can also expand this program with web push, mobile engage messages, SMS, or offline channels. Web push does not require an application, and you can send push notifications to mobile and desktop web browsers. If you have mobile applications, you can use mobile Engage to send push in app and inbox messages for your mobile app users.

### 23

发言人 06:50

### 24

To evaluate the effectiveness of this program, I add the value measurement node. A few days later, I can check the uplift generated by my programs on the Value Measurement dashboard. Now I check the participation, allowing contacts to enter this program only once ever. My program is ready for testing.

### 25

发言人 07:20

Everything looks good. So I activate the program. Once your program is active, it's important to analyze its performance. On the strategic dashboard, you can see the impact of your ongoing marketing strategies. The overview of each strategy helps you monitor your marketing activities and guide you towards achieving the desired objectives. I click on details of the reduced customer return widget to see the results of my winback defecting customers tactic. There are detailed reports for each program and the different campaigns within them.

### 27

发言人 07:58

_重复来源：10-Automation_原文.docx_

### 28

On the customizable Emarsys home, you can monitor your results and access more reports. I want to highlight further reporting related pages. The Revenue Analytics page helps you understand how your marketing activities contribute to your business results. On the event Attribution and Conversion report, you can create and measure the engagement of specific events, like adding a product to a wish list and buying it later.

### 29

发言人 08:26

### 30

Now let's talk about integrations. In order to sync your customer event and order data with a few clicks, we offer various integration solutions to Sap products or with our extensive partner ecosystem, including Eco. If you want to feed all the collected data into other applications, you can use open data, which is a Google Cloud Platform plugin. It allows you to query and export behavior and response data from different channels stored in the Sap m.r.s. platform. This concludes our overview. The SAP Emarsys customer engagement platform gives you many options to manage your account and user profile. Thank you for your attention.

### 2

### 4

Welcome In this video, we will introduce our automation solutions for creating omnichannel customer journeys. Both audience based and event based programs are built with multiple nodes, allowing you to solve your marketing challenges. In our use case, Linn was browsing my web shop and added a product to her cart. She left my page without completing the purchase because she found the shipping cost too high to encourage her to make a purchase, we will set up an abandoned cart program to send her messages with a free shipping voucher code. Please note that your automation setup may vary depending on your package or implementation.

### 5

发言人 00:45

_重复来源：Imp01_原文.docx；new20+21-mobile engagement management_原文.docx_

### 6

To create the program, I go to Automation Automation Programs and click Create Program. Here I have four options. Audience bases. Journeys are lifecycle programs such as win back, defecting customers. Journeys are driven by customer behaviors like our abandoned cart use case. Product focused journeys are based on product catalog changes like price drop or back in stock tactics generate presbuteros programs based on proven business objectives. I choose the event based journeys and select web extend event trigger.

### 7

发言人 01:20

_重复来源：new10-email analytics_原文.docx_

### 8

Since lens activities related to my website, I name my program and save it.

### 9

发言人 01:26

_重复来源：new15-web push_原文.docx_

### 10

Nodes are listed in two groups, actions and channels. AB test randomly splits your contacts according to the percentages that you define. With decision or decision splitter nodes, you define conditions to decide how a contact will proceed based on segments or events. With double opt in, you can send customers a message to confirm their subscription. This needs to be set up in your account first. Participation check controls how often your contacts can participate in programs and receive messages. Segment and Exclude let you add or remove contacts using any kind of segments. Filter switch splits contacts based on whether or not they are included in a given segment.

### 11

发言人 02:09

### 12

With the weight node, you can set a delay before contacts progress to the next node. The Wait for event node letsch react to incoming events within a time limit. Channel nodes trigger different types of marketing campaigns like email, web channel, or push channels like web channel or digital ads. Don't trigger messages, they add the contacts to an audience, so you have to remove them later.

### 13

发言人 02:33

_重复来源：3.2Management pages_原文.docx；new08-email campaign_原文.docx_

### 14

My program already has the entry node based on the trigger option. I chose earlier entry points to find how and when contacts can enter the program. I select the updated car event so contacts who updated their carts can enter my program. You can set a custom trigger event timeout to drop a particular event and stop executing the journey. If the message becomes irrelevant after a certain time, then I add a weight node and set it for 20 minutes, followed by a decision node so that only contacts who have not purchased or have added items to their cart can continue. I don't want to offer free shipping for all orders, so I use another decision node to check if L's cart includes products from my brand. Filtering events this way will help you send only relevant messages and avoid sending multiple emails within a short period.

### 15

发言人 03:35

### 16

Next, I add the participation check node to decide how contacts can continue their journeys. I offer the shipping voucher once to control the volume of messages. I could use the same participation check in multiple programs that target the same audience. Contacts not qualified to continue will exit the program, or I could design an alternative path for them.

### 17

发言人 03:58

_重复来源：9-Emarsys Scripting Language_原文.docx_

### 18

Let's include channel. First, I added Decision Splitter to ensure only marketing email subscribers receive my email campaign. I select include it in segment and choose this segment.

### 19

发言人 04:17

### 20

Then I add the send email node node and select my pre create a email campaign which I can edit or preview. Please note that in event based journeys, you can only send triggered emails a few days later. I want to send a reminder to contacts who have not completed their purchase. To do so, I add a wait for event node, select Webex, and then purchased. I set the timeout to two days and click Done For those contacts who did not complete the purchase, I add a web push node to show a browser push notification about their free shipping voucher. For contacts who have not subscribed to my marketing emails, I add a web channel campaign. I use a wait for event node. And remove the contacts from the web channel campaign from both paths.

### 21

发言人 05:20

### 22

Now that our program is ready, let's look at some additional nodes and features. If you have loyalty, you can create programs that trigger loyalty actions or assign rewards. With the update contact list, you can add contacts to or remove them from a contact list. With the webhook node. You can connect a Marxist to your offline channels, such as third party messaging platforms or distribution centers to add personalized Gifs or direct mail solutions.

### 23

发言人 05:47

### 24

When editing programs, you can disconnect and connect nodes, perform bulk actions, select entire paths or multiple nodes, and then move. Copy? Paste? Or delete them? Use Undo to restore your last action. Keyboard shortcuts are also available for these actions. You can add custom names. Send comments to your nodes and even tag your colleagues who will be notified in the platform or via email, depending on their notification settings.

### 25

发言人 06:33

### 26

Before activating the program, I want to test it. So I choose test. I set the test audience, skip certain nodes, override the time set in weight nodes, and ignore the wait for event or participation. Check node settings to avoid potential mistakes. Freeze your program before making changes in the test settings. Once I'm done testing, I schedule my program choosing Ongoing. And activate it.

### 27

发言人 07:07

_重复来源：6-campaign design-VCE_原文.docx；7-campaign design-VCE advanced_原文.docx_

### 28

Now let's look at the differences between event based and audience based on journeys. While event based journeys are for real time responses to events and actions, audience based journeys are more suitable for lifecycle engagement programs. In the audience based journeys, entry points are not predefined and you can choose from several of them. Transactional entry points add contacts to a program immediately upon meeting certain criteria without waiting for preset times or batching. Use the single node to start a one time batch program targeting a segment across channels on auto import to let the contacts of an auto import enter the program and run programs regularly with the recurring node. Connect programs with the entry from program and transfer to program nodes for more sophisticated journeys. You also have additional nodes and settings.

### 29

The value measurement node is used before channel nodes to measure the effect of your communications. This splits the audience into a treatment and control group. On the value measurement page, you find the results of all programs containing this node on the overview, your groups are compared by the number of contacts and their purchase and engagement rates. Below you have further widgets, one for each KPI. Exit criteria removes contacts from the program as soon as they meet the criteria of a segment. If you don't want to start from scratch, go to Tactics to generate presbuteros programs and use their titles and descriptions to help you choose the creative tactic is added to your automation programs. And can be customized to fit your needs?

### 30

发言人 08:46

### 31

You can see all your programs in the automation's overview page, along with information such as status or type. Use the filters to reduce the number of programs listed. If you have other accounts linked to this one, you can distribute programs to them. You can also check the program reporting, edit, copy or delete the program. Use the multi selection to bulk, delete, distribute and tag programs. With the Asset Tagging tool, you can categorize and arrange assets based on different criteria. Then you can use these tags on the automation page.

### 32

发言人 09:30

### 33

And create saved views. Let's check the reporting. I enter a time range and select a view for the chart. For event based programs, I can check contact level reports for a specific contact. In the History widget, you see how many contacts entered, the number of messages triggered, the number of contacts that finished and exited the program within the selected time frameme. Click here for more detailed reporting.

You can see the revenue generated by the program if you have smart insight or web extend. In this widget, you find the total revenue attributed to this program, the number of purchases made by contacts who participated in it, and the average order value. The Program Details widget provides an overview of your program numbers above. The nodes indicate how many contacts pass through. For more data, check the node details.

## 11.2-strategic analytics

来源文件：`11.2-strategic analytics_原文.docx`
本节跳过与前文重复的文本块：1 段。

### 1

11.2-strategic analytics_原文

### 2

发言人 00:05

_重复来源：new05-contact import_原文.docx；new09-autoflow_原文.docx_

### 3

Welcome Measuring the impact of your campaigns is crucial in order to optimize your marketing activities. In this video, we focus on strategic analytics that includes revenue attribution and revenue analytics, value measurement, audience reporting, campaign analytics, event attribution, and conversion reporting, and strategic dashboard. We cover further analytics topics in our smart Insight and new contact registrations and email analytics videos.

### 4

发言人 00:38

_重复来源：new15-web push_原文.docx_

### 5

First, let's see how we measure and attribute revenue. Your contact Jane receives a marketing campaign from you with a link to a product. A nice pair of jeans catches her eye and she clicks through to view them on your webshop Because our links have a unique tracking code, Jane can be identified and we follow her actions using Webex and a set of scripts on your website that collect all Jane's behavioral data. A few days later, Jane decides to buy one of the tee-shirts she saw. With our attribution settings, you can decide which marketing message the revenue of her purchase is attributed to. I used the last touch model, My attribution window is seven days and email click attribution is enabled. This means when Jane buys the t-shirt, look back at what email she received and click the past seven days and attribute the revenue to the last touch point she made if you have permission.

### 6

发言人 01:41

_重复来源：9-Emarsys Scripting Language_原文.docx；Imp08_原文.docx_

### 7

You can also enable the attribution on different channels and touch points. And you can change the sales data source. Choosing Smart Insight data allows for offline revenue to also be attributed.

### 8

发言人 01:53

_重复来源：Imp02_原文.docx；Imp03_原文.docx_

### 9

With this being said, let's check revenue analytics. This report shows the overall revenue impact of your marketing strategies and allows you to optimize your campaigns and programs. The chart on top shows the revenue based on your sales data in your default currency. Total revenue shows the sum of all purchases during the selected time period. Below that, you see the revenue attributed to a Marshes campaigns and the percentage of your total revenue that can be attributed based on your sales data uploaded to Emarsys. These metrics are updated every 4 hours. If you'd like to receive a detailed revenue impact report every week, just click the weekly Report button and activate your email subscription. By scrolling down, you can see your top performing programs and campaigns in terms of revenue.

### 10

发言人 02:43

### 11

Let's move on to value measurement. On this dashboard, you find the measured results of all programs containing the measurement node. At the top, you define the time period and select the programs you want to analyze. Keep in mind that programs can only be selected three days after activation.

### 12

发言人 03:02

### 13

In the overview, your treatment and control groups are compared by the number of contacts and their purchase and engagement rates. The measurement node automatically splits your contacts into 90% treatment and 10% control groups. In the KPI performance box is summarized how many Kpi's have a positive effect? Below you have further widgets, one for each KPI for deeper analysis, including a group comparison bar chart, and success indicators showing whether your experiment was successful. ## 2-Deliverability

来源文件：`2-Deliverability_原文.docx`
本节跳过与前文重复的文本块：1 段。

### 1

2-Deliverability_原文

### 2

2026年07月22日 18:18

_重复来源：3.1-Home page_原文.docx；3.2Management pages_原文.docx；4.1-Contacts1-Managing Data_原文.docx；4.2-Contacts2-Personalizationg and Segmentation_原文.docx；5-Forms_原文.docx；7-campaign design-VCE advanced_原文.docx_

### 3

发言人 00:15

_重复来源：5-Forms_原文.docx_

### 4

Welcome In this video, you will learn what deliverability is, how to build and maintain sender reputation, how to monitor and solve deliverability issues.

### 5

发言人 00:27

_重复来源：new17-mobile wallet1_原文.docx_

### 6

Deliverability is the ability to deliver emails to your recipients. The first thing you need to know about deliverability is that most major email platforms generate revenue from advertising. This means they benefit from keeping users on their platform, looking at their inbox for as long as possible. They achieve this by filtering out spam and unwanted email that will be ignored and filtering in the emails that are wanted and will be read. These three things influence your deliverability. Sending emails from a trusted and secure infrastructure, your existing sender reputation, which comes from a review of your previous campaigns, and your email campaigns themselves as both the email content and contact data are reviewed before your email is delivered.

### 7

发言人 01:17

_重复来源：4.2-Contacts2-Personalizationg and Segmentation_原文.docx_

### 8

A good sender reputation results in better inbox placement, but building a good reputation takes time. So when you start sending from SAP Emarsys, this is your first milestone. Your first email campaigns should focus on giving your new send domain a trusted sender reputation. To establish this reputation, start by sending small email campaigns to your most active customers, gently increasing the volume each send. Doing this for one to two weeks will give you the best start. After this, you can start phasing in the rest of your marketing streams until you have migrated your full email program to SAP Emarsys plan to increase volumes steadily each send, avoiding sudden changes in volume.

### 9

发言人 02:03

### 10

To maintain a good sender reputation, aim to be relevant to most of your recipients. Most of the time, this means you need to be aware of your email content, your recipients, and your sending frequencies. SAP Emarsys provides a wide range of tools to help you achieve this and improve your results. What are these tools?

### 11

发言人 02:21

### 12

Customer lifecycle letsgo segment in a way that will help your deliverability. Tactics and automated programs help you stay relevant in terms of timing, dynamic content, personalization, and using the right channels. The campaign check helps you prevent deliverability issues. Even before you send AB testing. Your campaigns supports you to raise response rates and improve deliverability.

### 13

As a marketer, you should continuously monitor your email results in order to detect and fix any deliverability issues. How can you do that in the Sap Mar platform? Engagement reporting shows you who is interacting with your emails to help you understand if you're targeting and segmenting correctly. Domain reporting is one of our deliverability tools that helps you identify possible issues with your deliverability on the ISP and email provider level. If a domain has an abnormally low click rate, it means that the click rate is significantly lower than expected, which could be a sign of a deliverability issue that needs investigation.

### 14

发言人 03:30

_重复来源：5-Forms_原文.docx_

### 15

Campaign analytics shows how your performance is changing over time with the ability to toggle date ranges, total values, and percentages. This can be very helpful to identify issues before they become a problem and check your own inbox too. Replies from your contacts can often show if you sending isn't going to plan. If something doesn't look right, how do you investigate and recover from delivery issues?

### 16

发言人 03:57

### 17

If you experience high bounce rates, check the detailed information which you'll find in the bounce management. Soft bounces are temporary blocks. Stop sending to these contacts for a few sends and retry it in a couple of weeks. Hard balances are invalid. Email Addresses You can prevent hard balances by having a secure opt in process and good data management, which means you remove expired and invalid addresses from your lists regularly. And block bounces are typically related to your sender reputation, which we've covered earlier. As well as bounces, you can get early warnings of potential future issues by looking for increases and unsubscribe rates, or by looking for dips in open and click rates. If you do see a change, check both campaigns and trends to see if this is a longer term issue or a sudden event that points to a specific campaign.

### 18

发言人 04:55

_重复来源：4.2-Contacts2-Personalizationg and Segmentation_原文.docx；Imp03_原文.docx_

### 19

If you do see a potential issue while you're investigating, it's often worth going back to your sender reputation building process and just sending to your most active contacts. This helps you get back in the inbox while you're investigating and resolving those issues.

### 20

发言人 05:13

_重复来源：new11-Joule_原文.docx_

### 21

Remember that it's easier to avoid deliverability issues than to recover from them. So keep checking your signup processes, review your customer data lifecycle, email strategy, and sending frequency. Because to be welcome in the inbox, you need to send relevant content at the right time frequency and to the right people. In any case, we are here to help. Check our documentation site. Our help portal provides you with access to our technical client service team. If you want help making improvements to your email marketing strategy, we offer a range of consulting solutions. Speak to your Client Success Manager for further details. We hope you found this video useful, thank you for your attention.

## 4.1-Contacts1-Managing Data

4.1-Contacts1-Managing Data_原文

### 2

### 3

Hi, there.

### 4

发言人 00:11

_重复来源：4.2-Contacts2-Personalizationg and Segmentation_原文.docx；8-HTML Editor&Triggered Email_原文.docx；9-Emarsys Scripting Language_原文.docx_

### 5

In this session, we'll be looking at how to manage your most important resource, your contact data. Data can get to Emarsys in a number of ways. API Webex integrations to eeco Merce platforms like Sap Commerce, Merce Cloud Magento or Shopify, relational data connections, Emarsys, forms, or file imports. You can use the data to create personalized messages to target segments. You can access the data on the platform via API or plugins and even export it.

### 6

发言人 00:44

### 7

Let's start by looking at how we add contact data to the platform. The Emarsys API is one of the easiest ways to synchronize data automatically between Emarsys and your other systems, allowing you to easily create and update contacts. The Emarsys JavaScript API, also called the Webex and is the Emarsys data collection module. It captures the activity and behavior information of the website visitors. With this information, you can enrich your Emarsys us contact database with web behavior fields and generate personalized product recommendations. The Emarsys relational data service enables you to use your business specific data on the SAP Emarsys customer engagement platform by connecting your external databases.

### 8

发言人 01:27

_重复来源：Imp06_原文.docx_

### 9

Now we'll cover data import, data export, and data management. Let's look at how you can use the Emarsys data import feature to add new contacts or update your existing ones, either with a manual import or automatically. On the data import page, you see three tabs, current imports, history, and auto imports. On the History tab, you see all previous imports and their details.

### 10

发言人 01:55

### 11

Let's start and import. Here, you'll see two options, create auto import and import file manually.

### 12

发言人 02:02

_重复来源：Imp02_原文.docx；new14-web channel_原文.docx_

### 13

Here is our use case. I have just had a new type of event in my flagship store, a bunch of new clients signed up to receive my newsletter, so I want to make sure they're in my database. Before you start and import, make sure all fields and values for single or Multichoice questions are available. Otherwise create them on the field editor page. I click Import file manually and drag and drop the contact file from my event into the highlighted area. In the import settings, I choose my unique identifier to avoid contact duplication in the database. In my case, this is email. The opt in declarations help you decide whether or not these contacts should be opted in.

### 14

I included a column with individual opt in values, so I choose in file. I select the registration language for these users and to avoid errors, I make sure the date format is consistent with my file. Next, I match the column headers in my file with the corresponding field names in my Emarsys contact database. And I'm ready to move on to the next step.

### 15

发言人 03:13

Contact data to include data fields from the Emarsys contact database with a predefined fallback text and formatting vouchers to add existing omnichannel voucher pools, external data to personalize your messages using data via API, for example, information on purchased products, relational data. ## 9-Emarsys Scripting Language

来源文件：`9-Emarsys Scripting Language_原文.docx`
本节跳过与前文重复的文本块：5 段。

### 1

9-Emarsys Scripting Language_原文

### 2

发言人 00:00

_重复来源：new20+21-mobile engagement management_原文.docx_

### 3

Jonathan, the word.

### 4

Hello, in this video you will learn the basics of the Emarsys scripting language, or as we call it, e.l.. This is an introductory video aimed at all Emarsys users, even those who have never written code before. Simply put, the Emarsys scripting language letsch create dynamic content in your campaigns.

### 5

发言人 00:33

_重复来源：new16-mobile first_原文.docx_

### 6

Although we Emarsys already provides out of the box personalization variables, conditional text, and block targeting solutions, with ESL, you build your own personalization rules, freeing you completely from any technical dependencies. You can create rules based on various data sources. This can be contact data like birth date, business specific data such as particular product information from your relational database, or any external values like additional customer data that you don't store in the platform. So the customization of your content becomes easy and fully automated with ESL. Before we dive into ESL, let's take a quick look at the basic components that come with the platform personalization, conditional text, and block targeting. With personalization variables, you can use your account system or custom fields to populate your content.

### 7

For example, you can include the first name and the city that was given by your contact. So your message reads, Hi Pierre, get free delivery all around Paris in emails. We also have predefined rules to display content based on conditions which you can apply to any text content. For instance, you can show specific content depending on the gender of the. Finally, you can use block targeting to hide or show an entire content block depending on the conditional type you choose. For example, you can use it to display a block with a 10% discount only for your inactive customers. But what if you'd like to create more complex dynamic content? This is where the Amasa scripting language comes in.

### 8

发言人 02:29

_重复来源：new08-email campaign_原文.docx_

### 9

ESL uses expressions, filters, and statements to build rules that will define what content appears in your message.

### 10

发言人 02:37

### 11

First, let's look at the terminology. What's an expression, a filter, and a statement? An expression is basically the data or output you'd like to show in your message, it looks like this, an expression can be a combination of many things, It can be a constant or something fixed, like the exact date when a voucher expires, or it can be a variable, like each customer's first name. ESL returns a value once you've given it an expression. See this, This is an expression that returns the value of George.

### 12

A filter is a particular rule for an expression use a pipe character when you want to include filters in the expression. I'll take the first name example again in case you don't have the customer's first name, you can display a fallback text by using the default filter. So if the first name field has no value in the system, display friend instead. It looks like this if you would like to have more filters for one expression in your HTML based email, you can combine them easily to show the value in uppercase and still have a fallback text type the following.

### 13

So far, so simple, right? A statement is a command given by you to the computer to take specific actions and is used to describe a behavior or a logic. In the case of an if statement, you set a condition that can be evaluated as true or false. For example, if gender equals male is true based on the result, you define the action. If gender equals male is true, write Mr last name.

### 14

发言人 04:30

### 15

A statement may have internal components, for example, expressions like a personalization field. In case you need to define a statement, start it with a curly bracket followed by a percent sign and close it with a percent and a closing curly bracket. Between these, you need an actual statement defining what happens in all possible cases.

### 16

发言人 04:54

_重复来源：new14-web channel_原文.docx_

### 17

Let me show you another example. If the first name of your contact has six characters or more, display, otherwise words, hello friend, otherwise display hello first name. For that, I need to use the filter called length, which counts the length of the word. It looks like this, so if your contact is called Jonathan, the word friend will be shown. But if the first name is just John, we can display that because it's only four characters long. If you'd like to combine the two previous examples in a statement, you need to start by checking if the first name has no value and then continue as before.

### 18

发言人 05:40

_重复来源：Imp04_原文.docx_

### 19

So today I showed you the basics of the Emarsys scripting language, how it compares to the existing personalization solutions, and the required terminology. ESL helps you easily include business specific and customer specific information to increase the re see of your content. To learn more about ESL, please check out our documentation pages. I hope that you enjoyed this session and thank you for your attention.

## Emarsys Reputation通用提高方法

来源文件：`Emarsys Reputation通用提高方法.docx`

### 1

Emarsys Reputation通用提高方法

### 2

按我们客户经验，如能保持发给活跃人群一般在一个月情况会改善，如偶尔会发不同人群的情况下，我们观察到需要的时间会较长，有些客户会需要多个月来完全修复。这个信誉因为完全由Google的系统，及发送的人群影响，我们无法直接控制到它的速度，只能靠按照Google提供的准则发优质的人群来提速，没有其他办法或捷径了

### 3

可以在活动1 － 2周前找我们咨询，提供活动的规模及发送次数就可以了

### 4

在信誉度稳定发送1 － 2周后，可按以下次序加入人群，再观察信誉及spam rate的变化1 - 2周，确认稳定时可继续增量:

### 5

90天内打开 (非privacy open) 及点击用户

### 6

90天内有下单用户

### 7

30天新注册用户

### 8

90天有访问网站用户

### 9

90天内打开 (privacy open)用户

### 10

完全没有任何活动的联系人不能大量添加到发送列表中，因为他们高机率投诉，这部分联系人需要准备reactivation邮件来激活他们（能吸引他们打开 / 点击的内容）。这一批用户每天可以发的量是当前每天发送量的5%

### 11

例: 如你们的每天发送量是20w, 你们每天可发1w邮件给不活跃联系人

### 12

如他们有打开／点击邮件，他们会自动进入日常的发送列表，如他们仍然不打开／点击邮件，则不建议再继续发邮件给他们了。你们也可选择由其他渠道触达并激活他们 (例: web push, digital ads -> 吸引用户再次访问网站 -> 触发flow邮件)

### 13

4. 发这种人群时的确需要小心处理，因为如人群内有过邮件互动行为的比例极低，代表该邮件的spam rate会被拉高。以下是一些避免这个情况发生的一些方法:

### 14

发送这种邮件时，同时安排发邮件给高互动的群组。利用高活跃用户的正面反馈来抵消低互动用户可能带来的负面影响，防止信誉被ISP瞬间降级。

### 15

如你们的分群有特定用户行为，应利用该数据进行高度个性化的触达。例如：提供对应产品的配套优惠、专属会员权益或售后关怀，而非通用广告。高相关性的内容能提高点击率、激活沉睡用户

### 16

将大群组拆分为小的批次发送，并实时监控退信率、投诉率和打开率。一旦发现指标异常（如进入垃圾箱比例上升），应立即停止发送并重新评估名单质量。

SAP Emarsys aims to offer a smooth onboarding experience. The setup process results in fully configured accounts so that customers can maximize engagement across channels. Collecting and integrating data for the account.

Enabling and configuring channels for sending messages.

Welcome In this video, we will provide an overview of the SAP Emarsys's customer engagement implementation steps. We'll cover the topics you have to consider before the implementation starts and the steps of the actual implementation as well. So what do you need to consider before you start the implementation? Identify the use cases. Do you need a multi language or multi currency setup? Are you considering a single or multi account system? How many brands do you plan to serve? What channels are you going to implement now and in the future? User identification is of key importance. Are there contacts consistently identified across channels?

### 17

Ideally you have already mapped out all data sources, data warehouses and architecture including CRM systems, plugins, and connections that have a connection to your Emarsys account. For real time user journeys, you will need Apis to be set. Have you thought over all the events that will trigger user messages and journeys in Emarsys or externally? Think about the deliverability requirements such as email sending compliance and the DNS settings, IP warm-up and timelines, and key elements like domains and dedicated reply management should also be considered. What content types will be used? Think of templates, HTML, images, fonts, and more. Also consider the data export from the Emarsys. Will the data be fed into a third party Bi tool Once you have a plan for all the above, your scoping is done and the implementation process can start.

The first step is provisioning to configure an A Emarsys account, also called tenant. A Emarsys needs some information tenant name to reflect the name of the business or company. For example, group admin for open data, loyalty related information. Once the account is set up, it's time for the account owner creation. Account owners are the users who can create and maintain all the user profiles and permissions, as well as manage the security settings in the account. Next, we have the Webex, then data collection scripts. These need to be deployed on the website to track and collect web behavior data, for example, which contact is viewing which products, which can later be used for product recommendations.

Lastly, sales data. This powers smart insight to help clients automate the lifecycle of their marketing journeys regarding data alignment. Use email addresses or customer Id's to link sales records to a Mars's contacts. The product information must be reconciled with sales data.

The planning and execution of a proper IP ramp up is key. Then send your first text messages so as soon as the account has been created and the data is prepared, you can upload your brand's creatives and set up the messages in all the desired channels. ### 9

Account Owner Roles and Responsibilities

### 10

Every SAP Emarsys account needs at least one Account Owner. The Account Owner is a super user or super admin who manages users, security settings, and single sign-on (SSO) configurations. Only Account Owners have access to the User Management and Security Settings pages. Existing Account Owners appoint new ones, or they're named during account creation.

### 11

Account Owners set the security levels for account access and data management, and handle user roles and permissions. Their responsibilities include:

### 12

Activating the Account Owner role.

### 13

Configuring the account security settings.

### 14

Setting up user roles and permissions.

### 15

Creating, editing, re-activating, deactivating, verifying, and deleting users and assigning roles to users in the account.

### 16

Enabling and setting up single sign-on (SSO).

### 53

Administrators with Specific Roles and Permissions

### 54

Roles and permissions define what features the user can safely access and use.

### 55

The account has four preset roles with set permissions to accommodate user needs, located in ‘Management > User Management > Roles.’

### 56

These default user roles are available:

### 57

Account Owner: This role has Account Owner capabilities, including managing users and security settings, and can be combined with others to offer wider access.

### 58

Administrator: This role gives full access to all SAP Emarsys features, except those features limited to Account Owners and Smart Insight.

### 59

Operator: Users in this role have access to essential features for daily marketing tasks, such as email campaigns, segments, and automation programs.

### 60

Restricted: This onboarding role has minimal access to features and products.

### 61

BI (Business Intelligence) Administrator: Users in this role have access to Smart Insight, which is relevant for accounts with this feature activated.

### 62

The default roles aren't modifiable, however, click the edit icon to view them. If they don't meet the wanted needs, duplicate and change one of them or create a new ‘empty’ role and activate all relevant permissions.

For example, an Email Campaign Manager needs full control over the email campaigns and templates, while an Analyst needs dashboard and reporting access. An Intern or Junior Marketer can view and edit campaigns. These permissions follow the SAP Emarsys platform's menu layout and related functionalities, such as editing.

The Security Settings page allows Account Owners to set security levels for account access and data management. The settings include:

### 120

Permitted email domains:

### 121

The Account Owner adds these email domains to enable users to receive activation and account-related emails, ensuring safe access. Each account needs at least one domain.

### 122

IP access control:

### 123

IP address restrictions and two-step authentication offer improved security measures to prevent illegal access even if login credentials are compromised. This restriction helps implement strong security protocols to protect sensitive information and keep system integrity.

### 124

Two-step authentication needs IP access control. Add IPs to the allowlist for letting users log in with a username and password. Users from unrecognized IPs must use two-step authentication. SAP Emarsys recommends enabling this feature and denies responsibility for illegal access.

API credentials:

### 126

It enables Account Owners to create, edit, delete, and individually activate or deactivate endpoint permissions for API users.

### 130

OpenID Connect (SAP Cloud Identity) creates API credentials using data available at SAP Cloud Identity (SCI).

### 131

WSSE only applies to customers with contracts signed before February 24, 2025.

Edit and enable the permissions for the chosen API endpoints. After making changes, click 'Save’ to apply them.

### 133

WebDAV users:

Most users prefer APIs or SFTP because they offer stronger security and greater flexibility, but WebDAV is a safe alternative. Keep in mind updating the WebDAV credentials regularly to improve security.

### 135

Keyring:

### 136

Key-based SFTP auto-imports make automatic data transfers from SFTP servers safe and simple. When using 4096-bit RSA keys, this feature improves security and simplifies data management processes.

### 137

Create and name the server authentication key and use the offered OpenSSH public key to configure the SFTP server. The key will then be available for auto-import setup, data transfer security, and remote source management.

### 138

Trying to access Security Settings without checking an email address can trigger a 'Forbidden' error. To resolve this problem, check the used email address in Management > User Management and follow the instructions in the confirmation email.

### 139

Account creation and setup: Customers need to offer configuration details before creating and setting up an SAP Emarsys account. The account configuration methods differ based on the contract. The SAP for Me portal or forms share the necessary information.

### 140

Account Owner roles and responsibilities: The Account Owner is a super user or super admin responsible for managing users, security settings, and Single Sign-On (SSO) configurations. Their responsibilities include activating the role, configuring security settings, and setting up user roles and permissions. It also manages users, enables, and sets up the SSO.

### 141

User management: This section covers creating new user roles, assigning roles to users, and managing user profiles. It also discusses the importance of tailored roles based on specific responsibilities and the ability to edit, duplicate, and delete roles.

### 142

Security settings: The Account Owner configures security settings, including multi-factor authentication and SSO. The lesson also highlights the importance of verifying user profiles and managing user access.

Notification emails: SAP Emarsys sends notification emails to keep users informed about important actions and updates, such as reports and data management updates. Notifications help maximize timely awareness and efficient management of platform activities.

### 3

Exploring Data Overview and Planning Strategy

### 4

Data Overview

### 5

Handling product, website, contact, and sales-related information in SAP Emarsys includes two main components: data onboarding and data management.

### 6

Data Onboarding involves integrating data from various sources into the platform. It includes the following steps:

### 7

Uploading product data, importing contact data, integrating web behavior data, and uploading sales data.

### 8

Mapping data to the correct fields and values to guarantee accuracy.

### 9

Verifying the data for completeness and correctness.

### 10

Configuring the use of the data across various channels.

### 11

This process guarantees correct data integration and readies it for use in SAP Emarsys. Depending on the business needs, different methods and tools are available to simplify the process, including using CSV files, Google Product Feeds, or the SAP Emarsys API.

### 12

Data Management focuses on organizing and improving the data for easy use. It includes:

### 13

Sorting data into specific groups.

### 14

Adding more details to contact profiles.

### 15

Studying the data to understand patterns and behaviors.

### 16

Making sure that the data is safe and compliant.

### 17

Connecting with other systems for consistent synchronization.

### 18

These steps help businesses use contact information to create tailor-made marketing campaigns and make data-driven strategic choices.

### 19

Datasets in SAP Emarsys

### 20

SAP Emarsys uses four core datasets to improve the experience for individuals who interact with the brand. These datasets include:

### 21

Contact data

### 22

Product data

### 23

Website data

### 24

Sales data

### 25

Each plays an important role in creating a unified contact profile and improving marketing strategies. Understanding how these datasets interact helps create a comprehensive data strategy to guarantee smooth data management and integration.

### 26

Contact Data offers a complete view of each contact's profile, helping businesses understand their contacts. External identifiers such as customer IDs are essential for creating a unique contact view. Businesses can use contact data fields for segmentation and personalization.​

### 27

Product Data allows for custom product recommendations, using identifiers such as product ID to refine contact interactions and maximize sales opportunities.​

### 28

Website Data tracks contact website behavior, helping businesses segment, automate, and customize marketing content across channels to drive sales and improve satisfaction and engagement. Recording this information needs external contact and product IDs.​

### 29

Sales Data focuses on contact purchasing history and spending habits. It allows businesses to tailor promotions and recommendations, making sure that marketing strategies are relevant, well-targeted, and actionable. This dataset includes external contact, product, and order IDs.

### 30

Overview of Datasets for SAP Emarsys Features and Channels

### 31

SAP Emarsys features access to different data types through internal connections within the SAP Emarsys account. Each feature uses specific data types. For example, Smart Insight and Emarsys Loyalty access contact, product, and sales data. Emarsys Loyalty, however, also uses its own database for extra information. Knowing which features need specific datasets helps plan effective data strategies and guarantee prerequisites such as correct Smart Insight setup.

### 106

SAP Emarsys improves contact identification by using contact, product, sales, and website information datasets. Contacts engage through registrations, purchases, website visits, and other activities. ### 121

Website Data and Web Extend

### 122

Web Extend is the SAP Emarsys data collection module, which tracks website visitors' interactions using cookies and explicit identifiers, such as external contact IDs. It ensures privacy with hashing and integrates with the contact database. This method of visitor tracking improves personalization while keeping data secure.

### 123

Contact identification using Web Extend also operates through several key processes:

### 124

When a website user successfully matches a contact, data collection scripts update the behavior fields. They link pseudonymized cookie information to that specific contact in SAP Emarsys.

### 125

When the contact engages with the website, the system logs and syncs all future interactions with the SAP Emarsys contact database. This process happens even if the user doesn't log in. Browser cookies identify the user and infer explicit identification.

### 126

Web Extend identifies users on several devices, such as desktops and mobile devices. It links cookies to a single identifier when a contact logs in, registers, or clicks through an email, allowing scripts to track and log all future visits.​

### 127

Other Web Extend benefits:

### 128

Sends data to SAP Emarsys, which allows web behavior-based segmentation.

### 129

Triggers SAP Emarsys Interactions programs based on Web Extend events.

### 130

Allows relevant website recommendations.

### 131

Website tracking JavaScript commands:

### 139

SAP Emarsys uses client-defined product identifiers to link products on the website with products on the platform. It enables Web Extend to track product views, purchases, and cart abandonments. The system matches identifiers from items the contact purchases, or adds to the cart, with identifiers in the product catalog. This enables personalized recommendations based on browsing history and product categories. Marketers use Predict to customize campaigns in Email, Web Channel, and Mobile Engage (apps). The product catalog also helps Smart Insight by linking sales data to prominent purchases.

Availability

Current (available) and historical products (for example, the item temporarily sells out, but it becomes available again in the future)

Google Product Feed​

CSV (the SAP Emarsys customer hosts the CSV file on their URL)

When setting up a new product feed for SAP Emarsys, leave fields empty when they don't have a value, and include the field names in the first row.​

### 153

To add a custom field in CSV format, include c_ at the start of the field name (for example, c_colour).​

### 154

Add up to five custom labels in Google Product Feed format. Without renaming, number them 0–4 (for example, custom_label_0, custom_label_1).

Multi-locale requirements for product data:

### 161

The default stock status must be true

### 163

Web Extend commands/content must match default values (price, category, item)

SAP Emarsys uses sales data to employ dynamic lifecycle segmentations. These segmentations aim to boost acquisition, purchasing frequency, average order value, and retention. This method enables quick implementation of customer experiences, leading to measurable success.

Web Extend data collection scripts: The lesson explains the functionality of Web Extend data collection scripts in tracking website interactions and processing data for SAP Emarsys applications. It highlights the importance of keeping a clean, compliant database and outlines key steps to consider before implementing these scripts.

### 194

E-commerce commands: Follow the instructions to implement e-commerce JavaScript commands that track visitor behavior, identify contacts, and manage data uploads. It includes adding commands to various website pages to report visitor actions such as viewing products, browsing categories, and completing purchases.

It also details the use of cookies to identify known visitors and link them with contacts in the database.

### 196

Integration and verification: The lesson offers instructions for confirming the integration of data collection systems and regularly checking the integration health on the Predict Data Sources page. It also suggests using tools like JSFiddle to test commands and the Live Events viewer to verify implementation.

### 3

Handling Essential Data Input

### 4

Uploading Data

### 5

To work effectively with SAP Emarsys, begin by adding and configuring data in the system. Each data type needs a specific upload method and a set of guidelines. This lesson organizes those methods by data type.

### 6

Contact Data Fields in SAP Emarsys

### 7

Before uploading contact data to SAP Emarsys, managing and defining contact data fields is essential. Each value links to a specific field. To store data correctly, verify that a proper field matches during the CSV import. This process helps avoid errors or misclassification. This step is critical for keeping data integrity. It also enables effective segmentation and personalization. Always verify that all necessary fields exist in SAP Emarsys before importing data.

### 8

The Field Editor shows details, such as IDs, for all account fields, including both system and custom fields, and allows users to create new fields and manage existing ones.

### 9

Contact Data: SAP Emarsys System Fields

### 10

Each SAP Emarsys account encompasses standard system fields in its database. These fields include details of its data type and field IDs. These elements are important for automating data transfers between SAP Emarsys and other applications. They play a critical role in core marketing functions to improve targeting. Marketers commonly use system fields such as first name, gender, country, and date of birth. These fields help tailor content and campaigns to individual contacts.

### 11

SAP Emarsys defines system fields that users are unable to create or change. However, users can make a copy of these fields, which they can customize.

### 12

Let's explore some specific system fields.

### 13

Special system fields to highlight:

### 14

Opt-in field (ID = 31): It stores permissions for email communications. SAP Emarsys allows updating this field only through a confirmed API process, not through forms or imports. Under specific conditions, campaigns proceed without requiring an explicit opt-in.​​

### 15

Do not track me field (ID=89705): It prevents Web Extend from using external contact IDs to track browsing and purchasing events. It also gives contacts the option to opt out of website data processing. Web Extend tracks behavior and sends data to Predict servers without external contact IDs. This data helps build statistical models for Predict recommendations.​

### 16

Date of birth field (ID=4): It supports birthday campaigns, but entries often lack reliability or are empty because people sign up with incorrect data. The system ignores birthdays over 120 years and invalidates empty fields. Contacts offering a correct month and day but a 19th-century year to hide their age don't qualify for birthday campaign benefits.​

### 17

IETF language tag field (ID=89707): This field uses client-loaded language codes per contact to enable language-specific communication. It doesn't include set values, but it needs to follow one of these formats based on the context:

### 18

"en" for English, "de" for German.

### 19

"en-US" for English (United States), "en-GB" for English (Great Britain).

### 20

VCE multi-language campaigns demand the latter format. To verify the language tags, open a VCE email, navigate to the Language Versions tab, and copy the master version.

### 21

Contact Data: Custom Fields

### 22

SAP Emarsys users create custom fields through the application interface or by using the API to improve segmentation, personalization, and contact data management across forms, segments, and campaigns. Choosing the correct field type is important for accurate data handling and functionality. Make sure date formats are consistent.

### 23

Supported field types are:

### 24

Numeric

### 25

Short Text

### 26

Long Text

### 27

Unlimited Text

### 28

Date

### 29

Single Choice

### 30

Multiple Choice

### 31

URL

### 32

Best practice: SAP Emarsys recommends creating custom fields for the following purposes:

### 33

Contact identification: If using a custom external contact ID as an identifier, create a custom text-entry alphanumeric field to store this data.

### 34

Newsletters: Create a custom field to capture newsletter preferences, especially when offering various newsletter options.

### 35

Original registration date: Employ a custom field to store the original registration date, as SAP Emarsys only displays the date a contact entered its system, which differs from their actual sign-up date with the client’s brand.​

### 36

Special Custom Field Types

### 37

SAP Emarsys adds special custom fields (admin fields) to an account when activating specific SAP Emarsys products.

### 38

Web behavior fields: The Web Extend data collection scripts update custom fields prefixed with ‘predict’ based on website visitor activity every few hours. These fields support use cases such as cart abandonment campaigns and cross-category upsell strategies.

### 39

SAP CX integration fields: SAP CX connectors include a predefined set of mapped fields between the SAP Emarsys and SAP CX systems, such as the SAP Customer Data Platform. Prefixes such as CD_id, CD_C_, CD_S_, CD_P_, and CD_D_ organize consent, subscription, and profile data.

### 40

Channel-specific fields: Enabling certain channels adds corresponding fields to the account. For example, activating the SMS channel introduces the SMS Opt-in field, while Mobile Engage includes the App-Installed field to support multi-channel engagement.

### 41

Legacy Features Regarding Contact Data

### 42

Interests field (ID = 0): This field is a default multi-choice option. Set its values under Management > Form Settings before activation. Once configured, the field shows automatically in SAP Emarsys forms to capture registration preferences. Remove the field from the form if it doesn't serve the wanted purpose.

### 43

Forms: SAP Emarsys offers built-in forms for collecting and updating contact data. Users can create sign-up or targeted newsletter forms to deploy on websites or messages such as Web Channel and Mobile In-app. Available types include General Registration, Contact Us, and Change Profile.

### 44

Voucher fields: These fields belong to the legacy voucher system. Replace this approach with the new Omnichannel Voucher Management, as SAP Emarsys now promotes and transitions to the newer solution.

### 45

Caution

_重复来源：Imp07_原文.docx；Imp08_原文.docx_

### 46

SAP Emarsys recommends against using these legacy features, even though they're still available. Think carefully before enabling or recommending these functionalities.

### 47

Contact Data Upload

### 48

Once the data files are ready, upload them to SAP Emarsys. The system then allows checking whether they meet the format requirements. After that, schedule regular updates. SAP Emarsys scans for contact duplication whenever it imports a new contact. The system automatically merges contacts with the same unique external contact identifier upon import.

### 49

Preparation stage:

### 50

Before starting the data import to SAP Emarsys, keep these considerations in mind:

### 51

Identify which field serves as the unique external contact identifier for identification and deduplication. SAP Emarsys technical representatives can index custom fields, enabling the selection at the import.

### 52

Determine which contact properties to include.

### 53

Verify that all needed custom fields exist in the SAP Emarsys account before importing data.

### 54

When importing CSV files into SAP Emarsys, ensure that the files have a newline control character in the first 265 KB, and use a period as the decimal separator.

### 55

Use consistent identifiers:

### 56

Align contact imports with a consistent, unique external contact identifier (for example, a generated, persistent alphanumeric string that is non-guessable and non-sequential).​

### 57

When importing from third-party systems, map external contact identifiers by creating a matching external contact ID field in SAP Emarsys.

### 58

Use the email address only if necessary, as using emails or guessable identifiers can increase security risks.​

### 59

Keep data quality:

### 60

Remove duplicate contacts before importing, ensuring accurate profile unification and avoiding engagement issues.

### 61

Keep contact data up to date:

### 62

Keep accuracy by updating contact data regularly, using:

### 63

The SAP Emarsys contact data API endpoints

### 64

Auto-Import feature

### 65

Extend functionality through an integration:

### 66

Use SAP Emarsys custom integrations to connect with leading e-commerce platforms, such as Shopify.

### 67

Occasional Data Upload: Contact Data Through Manual Import

### 68

​Customers usually upload contact data manually during the initial account setup. However, manual import is also an option for occasional contact data updates.

### 69

Follow these steps to upload contact data to the SAP Emarsys account:

### 70

Open the SAP Emarsys account.

### 71

Navigate to Contacts > Data Import.

### 72

Click on Import to start the Import Wizard.

### 73

Data importing process:

### 74

Make sure that the file meets the CSV guidelines and SAP Emarsys standards.

### 75

In the import wizard, choose a unique external contact identifier. Upon creation, the system generates an Internal contact ID for each record, which serves as a primary identifier in SAP Emarsys internal processes.​

### 76

Confirm that the file's data correctly maps to the fields and values in SAP Emarsys.

### 77

The import duration depends on the number of contacts and the complexity of the database field.

### 78

Regular contact data update: Contact data through API (Recommended):

### 79

Use the SAP Emarsys REST API to keep up-to-date contact data consistently.

### 80

Account Owners can create API users and save the API secret, as it shows only once.

### 81

Follow the guide to get a JSON Web Token (JWT) through OAuth 2.0 (See the note).

### 82

Include the JWT in the HTTP authorization header when making API calls to SAP Emarsys.

### 83

When using the SAP Emarsys API, key_id refers to the chosen unique external contact identifier.

### 84

SAP Emarsys experts recommend using the PUT request to update contacts. If the contact doesn’t yet exist in the SAP Emarsys account, it creates a new contact record.​

### 85

Test the connection with a staging website and/or test contacts.

### 86

Regular contact data Update: Contact data through auto-import - SFTP or WebDav:

### 87

Go to Contacts > Data Import.

### 88

To create an auto-import configuration, click Import File.

### 89

Then complete the following steps:

### 90

Define the name of the SAP Emarsys contact list. After each successful auto-import, the system saves the imported contacts as a contact list using the given name followed by a timestamp.

### 91

Select the file source: SFTP or WebDAV. Use an end–user–hosted server for SFTP. Use the SAP Emarsys WebDAV folder for WebDAV—an SAP Emarsys expert can configure access if needed.

### 92

Set the import file name. SAP Emarsys processes only files that match the specified naming convention. Use wildcards to handle dynamic elements such as timestamps.

### 93

To map the fields, upload a sample file. During the match values step, pay attention to single- and multi-choice fields and their values.

### 94

Select the correct unique external contact identifier to guarantee consistent removal of duplication during each import.

### 95

Review the summary and complete the setup.

### 96

The API allows a real-time method for updating contact data.

### 97

The opt-in status (field ID = 31) can only change from FALSE to TRUE through the API or by manually editing the contact record in the account. This restriction helps prevent compliance issues and reputation risks caused by incorrect updates through manual or automated imports.

### 98

SFTP and WebDAV need 15 minutes to 1 hour to process updates.

### 99

Use UTF-8 encoded CSVs only for files imported through SFTP and WebDAV.

### 100

Contact Deletion

### 101

SAP Emarsys allows contact deletion across all contact lists. It also removes related data such as Loyalty rewards and Smart Insight purchase history. This action erases historical behavioral and revenue data, affects contact-level reporting and potentially changes metrics across dashboards, analytics, and Smart Insight. To ensure full GDPR compliance, the platform completes deletion within up to one month.

### 113

Product Data Guidelines

### 114

General guidelines:

### 115

Use a simple .csv table format, with each row containing all the attributes of a product, including:

### 116

The item ID

### 117

The URL of the product page

### 118

A link to a product image, and so on.

### 119

Use a UTF-8 encoded CSV.

### 120

Include the field names in the first row (header) and make sure that all the following rows list the values corresponding to the fields in the header.

### 121

Do not use more than 255 columns.

### 122

Make sure the file list includes only letters, numbers, underscores, dashes, and slash characters. SAP Emarsys uses the slash solely for the g/prefix in the Parent/Child feature. ​

### 123

File size:

### 124

Make sure the file has fewer than a million items. If there are more, contact an SAP Emarsys expert.

### 125

If the size of the product is large (for example, several hundred megabytes in size), compress the data file with GZIP and update the URL so that the file has the .gz suffix.

### 126

How to upload product data:

### 127

Get the product data file ready and upload it to SAP Emarsys. Make sure it meets format requirements, then schedule regular updates.

### 128

Steps to upload product data for the first time:

### 129

Navigate to Management > Predict Data Sources.

### 130

To manage all product data tasks, access the product data page by clicking 'Manage product data' in the product data box.

### 131

The initial upload validates that the data format is correct before continuing with the first upload.

### 132

Choose one of the two upload methods.

### 133

Upload from a URL:

### 134

Choose from Google Product Feed (GPF) or CSV file format.

### 135

Select the file format in the settings box.

### 136

Google Product Feed:

### 137

Enter URLs and format settings for each location (various countries/regions supported).

### 138

2. SAP Emarsys CSV:

### 139

SAP Emarsys end users host the CSV file using an accessible URL on their server. SAP Emarsys supports safe connections using a username and password and offers formatting in the SAP Emarsys guidance.

### 140

Paste the full URL of the product data file in the URL field to schedule updates.

### 141

Set the frequency for updating the product data.

### 142

Click 'Save' and 'Update from URL now.'

### 143

Insert the URL in SAP Emarsys to trigger updates that run based on the frequency specified during setup.​

### 144

Make sure that the product file is regularly updated with recent data, so SAP Emarsys can collect it and deliver recommendations correctly.​

### 145

3. Upload the CSV product data file from a computer:

### 146

Compress CSV files that are larger than 100 MB, while handling smaller ones separately.

### 147

Click 'Upload file to validate' in the 'Manual update and validation' section.

### 148

Browse for the product data CSV file.

### 149

Confirm and publish product data:

### 150

Validation checks happen post-upload.

### 151

The system lists warnings with descriptions and highlights the relevant fields. To fix problems and resubmit, click 'discard this update.'

### 152

Click 'Activate update' to publish data if there are no warnings. Status updates appear in the current product data box.

### 153

Check the published product data:

### 154

Use the product search box to search for products in the client’s SAP Emarsys database.​

### 155

Verify product data import success on the product info page.

### 156

Confirm which GPF fields the system holds during conversion and how they appear in the SAP Emarsys product database after submitting a GPF product data file.

### 157

Force product data updates:

### 158

Manually update product data if a scheduled update fails or rejects it.

### 159

Click 'Update from URL now' and 'Activate update' to update the product data.

### 160

A manual update doesn't change the upload schedule. SAP Emarsys continues scheduled updates.

### 161

Use protocols such as SFTP or FTPS for hosting CSV files:

### 162

SFTP (SSH File Transfer Protocol)

### 163

FTPS (FTP over SSL/TLS). These URLs must start with ftps://.

### 164

Allowlist ScarabBot/1.0 user agent for URL scheduling.

### 165

Format URLs for password-protected storage as follows:

### 166

https://username:password@mywebshop.com/catalog.csv

### 167

sftp://username:password@mywebshop.com/catalog.csv

### 168

When importing product data files into SAP Emarsys, the system automatically removes redundant white spaces unless you quote them.

### 169

Maximize the GPF product data image size:

### 170

Image enhancement guarantees efficient viewing and prevents large images from slowing webpage loading.

### 171

The system enables image enhancement by default.

### 172

Enabled: Automatically maximizes images for show, possibly causing distortion.

### 173

Disabled: Images wider than 800 px don't appear. Reduce the size using the SAP Emarsys image proxy.

### 174

SAP Emarsys enables three out-of-the-box Tactics that use Product Data and Web Extend triggers:

### 175

Price drop

### 176

Back in stock*

### 177

Low in stock*

### 178

*For 'Back in stock' and 'Low in stock,' include a stock amount as a custom field in the product file. Set thresholds to identify when the system considers an item 'low in stock.'

### 179

Implement Web Extend Data Collection Scripts

### 180

Web Extend data collection scripts actively track visitors' interactions on the website. The data processing confirms the information. Then it gives this confirmed data to SAP Emarsys applications such as Smart Insight and Predict.

### 181

SAP Emarsys supports the maintenance of a clean, compliant database by encouraging adherence to data protection rules.

### 182

Consider the following steps before starting the implementation:

### 183

Web Extend scripts operate asynchronously, guaranteeing they don't affect the website's speed and performance.

### 184

Web Extend scripts handle contact data anonymously or pseudonymously, linking web behavior with actual contacts only after importing the data into the SAP Emarsys database.

### 185

Web Extend scripts collect data on all current and future web pages, including new products and landing pages for marketing campaigns. Add the relevant commands to the templates used for creating different page types.

### 186

Web Extend scripts integrate into dedicated mobile websites or mobile apps.

### 187

Implementation steps for data collection focus on identifying and tracking the contact's website behavior.

### 188

Add the account-specific tracking code to the website:

### 189

Integrating the Web Extend tracking code enables the tracking of visitor behavior using JavaScript API commands. These commands are important for identifying contacts across devices and tracking the success of campaigns. Go to Management > Predict Data Sources > Web Behavior and click Show tracking code.

### 190

Identify contacts:

### 191

SAP Emarsys employs cookies by default to monitor website visitors. These cookies identify known visitors, such as logged-in contacts or visitors who offer their email addresses and link them with contacts in the database.

### 192

setCustomerId OR setEmail: Use this command on pages that identify contacts. The type matches the unique contact identifier (setCustomerId for an external contact ID, setEmail for email).

### 193

Implement e-commerce JavaScript commands :

### 194

The prerequisites include:

### 195

Select a base currency for the account.

### 196

Upload the product data file.

### 197

Discuss the data collection and contact identification policy with an SAP Emarsys expert before implementing Web Extend scripts.

### 198

E-commerce commands:

### 199

These scripts send website data to the correct database locations for reporting, recommendations, predictions, and lifecycle management.​

### 200

Add the following commands to ALL pages on the website:

### 201

Cart: Reports visitors' cart content.

### 202

Go: Executes the commands in the queue.

### 203

Add the following commands to specific pages on the website:

### 204

View: Add to product pages. Reports the ID of the product that the website visitor views on the product page.​​

### 205

Category: Add to category results pages. Reports the category path that the website visitor browses.​

### 206

Purchase: Add to the purchase confirmation page. Reports customers' orders, offering a basis for earning statistics in SAP Emarsys, or identifying whether a contact completed a purchase.​

### 207

Use JSFiddle to see these commands in action. Verify the implementation by using the Live Events viewer in the Web Behavior box on the Predict Data Sources page.

### 208

5. Complete the steps and confirm the integration:

### 209

The system constantly collects data and enriches contact information through behavior fields.

### 210

Visit the Predict Data Sources page regularly to check integration health, especially after front-end updates, to guarantee that data collection scripts keep intact.

### 211

6. Other Web Extend commands:

### 212

Several other types of Web Extend commands exist. Implement these commands based on the use case, although they aren't required for standard SAP Emarsys functionality.

### 213

SearchTerm: Add to search results pages. Reports visitors' search terms entered in the website search box.​

### 214

Tag: This function adds a tag to the current event with optional attributes (defined by the SAP Emarsys user). The user later accesses this tag from other SAP Emarsys products. There are three different types, depending on the use case.

### 215

TestMode: Allows testing of commands without recording the results in SAP Emarsys.

If a step is specific to one of these cases, it will be tagged in the video.
